```
NEW THING                                  | TYPE              | HAPPY                          | FAILURE                               | EDGE
-------------------------------------------|-------------------|--------------------------------|---------------------------------------|-------------------------------
Plan choice at activation (FR-TA-182)      | integration + E2E | Fixed chosen, period opens     | payment method fails -> stays Trial    | activation on the 31st
Plan switch / cancel                       | integration       | switch from next 1st           | cancel after start -> 409             | two tabs submit
MonthlyRating by plan (US-9.8, 9.9)        | integration       | full Fixed month = fee         | gap day -> held + alert (R3-E1)        | Active on 17th: 15/31 days
Fixed over-allowance debit                 | integration       | 1,001st WhatsApp debited       | balance 0 -> email fallback            | two sends cross 1,000 together
Seat map publish (US-4.6, 4.7)             | integration       | template map goes live         | seat in use -> 409                     | zero seats with Seat choice on
Auto-assign seatless bookings (R3-A1)      | integration       | 3 bookings get distinct seats  | day with more bookings -> rejected     | bookings on many days
Publish vs hold race (R3-D1)               | integration       | (both orders)                  | hold on removed seat fails cleanly     | paused-pause-release schedule
Seat hold with picked seats (US-6.4)       | integration + E2E | A1 held 10 min                 | A1 taken during OTP -> 409 + map       | stale seat id from old map
Change seat (staff)                        | integration       | moved, customer notified       | target taken -> 409                    | move to same seat
Seat picker list view (NFR-A11Y-10)        | E2E (axe + keys)  | pick by keyboard               | screen reader announces lost seat      | 40 x 60 map
Seat label / text input                    | unit              | label saved                    | too long / control chars rejected      | unicode labels
```

Assertions and no_claim:
- Rating by plan: asserts the invoice line set equals exactly one FixedPlan line or exactly one line per server meter, never both; no_claim=real GST filing (invoice format reviewed by a CA separately).
- R3-E1: asserts a held invoice, one Finance alert naming tenant and days, and other tenants billed; no_claim=alert delivery to a real inbox (mocked notifier).
- R3-A1: asserts every future seatless booking ends with a distinct active seat, or publish fails with nothing changed; no_claim=none beyond protects.
- R3-D1: asserts that across both completion orders no allocation references an archived seat; no_claim=none beyond protects (real PostgreSQL via Testcontainers, controlled pause points).
- Seat picker: asserts axe clean and keyboard pick works; no_claim=real screen-reader voice output (manual pass in the launch checklist).

2am Friday test: book the last free seat from two phones at once while the owner publishes a map; exactly one booking succeeds, nobody sits on a removed seat. Hostile QA test: send a seatId from another product or tenant. Chaos test: kill the app server mid-publish; the transaction leaves the old map live.

Pyramid: mostly unit and integration on real PostgreSQL, few E2E. Flakiness risk: the race test depends on ordering; it uses explicit pause/release points, not sleeps.

No new verification choices: every test above proves an accepted requirement or an approved remedy (R3-A1, R3-E1, R3-D1).

### Section 7: Performance

- OK. Seat availability: one query on BookingAllocation for the product and time range (GiST index on unit and range), at most 2,400 seats; short per-map cache; meets NFR-PERF-07 on paper, to be measured in the week-14 performance pass.
- OK. Auto-assign at publish touches only that product's future seatless bookings; worst case a few hundred rows in one transaction.
- OK. Over-allowance pricing reads the Fixed allowance from the rate card in force (cached per month), adding no query to the atomic debit.
- OK. Rating adds one TenantPlanPeriod read per tenant.

No issues found.

### Section 8: Observability

Required at launch (Prime Directive 5; no behavior change):
- Logs: plan chosen/switched/cancelled (tenant, from, to, starts_on, user); rating outcome per tenant with plan and hold reason; seat map publish result (version, seats added/removed, auto-assigned count, rejection reason); seat hold conflicts (product, seat, reason).
- Metrics: seat-hold conflict rate; map publish failures by reason; Fixed tenants over any allowance this month; held invoices by reason (metering, missing plan, rate card).
- Alerts: invoice held for a missing plan period (R3-E1); any held invoice on the 2nd of the month.
- Runbook: "Invoice held: missing plan period" (find the gap, repair the period, re-run rating for that tenant); "Owner can't publish map" (read the rejection list).
- Debuggability: a seat complaint three weeks later can be rebuilt from BookingEvent (seat assigned, moved) and the map version history.

No new choices from this section.

### Section 9: Deployment & Rollout

- OK. Greenfield: plans and seat maps ship with launch; there is no live data to migrate and no old code running alongside.
- OK. Seat maps need no feature flag: a product without a map behaves exactly as before (Seat choice Off).
- WARNING R3-P1. **The week-20 launch date rests on an unproven pace** (P-R2-4). 14-build-plan.md assumes about 76 developer-weeks where the full-team plan used about 150, so each developer must produce about twice as much with Claude Code. Published evidence shows a moderate gain at best. The plan already says to re-plan after sprint 3, but it doesn't say what triggers a change or what launch tenants are told.

## Answered: R3-P1 (D10)
Approved: week 20 target with a week-4 checkpoint ("Week 20 target, checkpoint at week 4 (recommended)", 2026-11-05). Launch tenants are told "launching week 20–24". At the end of week 4 the team must demo the first paid booking end to end (sprint 3 goal in 14 §4). If it isn't demoed, the team re-plans from the measured pace and the owner chooses between moving the date and cutting from 14 §6. The production entry gate never moves.

### Section 10: Long-Term Trajectory

- Debt: four deferred items (R3-C2 to R3-C5) are tracked in TODOS.md with dates. Operational debt: until the cost view ships, SSPL checks Fixed-tenant cost by hand (runbook task T5).
- Path dependency: TenantPlanPeriod makes a third plan or annual billing a new period type, not a rewrite. Seats as ProductUnits keep a later floor-photo background or free-form layout a rendering change only.
- Knowledge: the plan rules live in Rule #12 and BRD §7; seat rules in "Seat maps (revision 2)" and FR-TA-068 to 068e; 14-build-plan.md maps both to sprints.
- Reversibility: plans 4/5 (a plan can be retired by stopping new choices and migrating periods); seat maps 4/5 (Seat choice Off returns a product to a plain pool after its future seat bookings end).
- 1-year question: a new engineer can follow it, provided the CLAUDE.md in the code repository points at these documents (14 §8 already requires that).

No new choices from this section.

```
Customer: date -> duration -> time -> Choose your seat (map | list) -> OTP -> hold -> pay -> pass shows seat

```

| Seat picker | map skeleton | product has no map: only "Any available seat" | seat taken: refreshed map + message | seat shown gold, then on pass | some seats out of service (hatched) |

Findings: OK on states and responsive intent (builder works by touch; picker has list view and zoom). The visual design of the builder and picker is not specified yet beyond these states; DESIGN.md is a Sprint 0 deliverable. Consider running /plan-design-review on the seat map builder and picker before week 8.

# Design Review 3 (plan-design-review, 2026-11-05): revision 2 screens

Target: the branch diff (pack v0.4): plan choice and the billing plan panel (FR-TA-182, FR-TA-175), the two-plan pricing page (FR-MW-002/003), the seat map builder (FR-TA-068 to 068d), the customer seat picker (FR-BA-013), Change seat (FR-TA-068f). No DESIGN.md (Sprint 0, 22A). Mockups not generated: the gstack designer has no OpenAI key on this machine. Focus: all 7 dimensions (D1). Outside voices: Claude subagent only (D2; Codex not installed, `[single-model]`).

Step 0 rating: 4/10. The plan states what each screen does and lists its states (CEO review 3, Section 11), but not what users see first, how painting works on a phone, where the seat step sits in the 2A checkout, or how the plan screen helps an owner choose. A 10 would add, per screen: hierarchy, touch model, layout per breakpoint, state copy and tokens.

Existing leverage: 1A slot picker, 2A checkout order, 17A desk pass, 23A colour roles, 26A admin on phones, 27A countdown, 29A tenant voice, 32A no card stacks, DR2-1A billing order, DR2-2A sample bills, DR2-3A billing states, shadcn/ui on Radix (22A).

Factual corrections applied (each follows an earlier approved decision; no new design choice): 5A and 28A marked superseded in part by revision 2; FR-TA-176's "no plan upgrade, downgrade" sentence now points to FR-TA-182; FR-TA-068c and US-4.7 now point to Change seat (FR-TA-068f) instead of the deferred FR-TA-068e; the design doc's seat map section marks "Seat us together" and Today on the map as after launch (R3-C4); the seat map performance row renamed NFR-PERF-07 (NFR-PERF-06 was used twice).

Outside voice (CLAUDE SUBAGENT, design completeness, single-model): 4/10. Critical: phone builder contradicts 26A and has no touch model; auto-labels unstable once seats have bookings; stale approved decisions (corrected above). High: seat step layout and fast path; 40 × 60 map on a phone; plan choice hierarchy and recommendation; pricing page as a generic two-card grid; publish sends customer messages without a preflight; seat colours vs tenant theme. Medium: Change seat details; builder save/limit states; plan panel copy; cabins and rooms on the map; motion.

## Design Review 3 decisions

(Each issue below is decided individually; pending items are not part of the plan until answered.)

**DR3-1A Pricing page as one comparison table (FR-MW-002/003, DR2-2A; D3).** First screen: the "0% commission" headline, one supporting line, and one primary button "Start free trial". Then one table: rows are SSPL's sample space profiles (for example 20, 60 and 150 seats with their page views, bookings and messages), columns Fixed and Usage, each cell the monthly total + GST from the SlabCalculator, the cheaper cell in each row marked "Lower". The rate tables (Fixed fee and allowances, slab tables per meter) sit folded below. No plan cards.

**DR3-2A Plan choice screen (FR-TA-182, US-9.8; D4).** Plan first, then payment method. Top line: "At your trial usage: Usage about ₹X/month · Fixed ₹F/month" (trial usage projected to a month by the SlabCalculator, + GST). Then two plain rows, Fixed and Usage, each with its price, what it includes and its message allowance or slab note; nothing preselected. A "Lower for you" tag appears only when one plan is more than 20% cheaper on that projection. One line under the rows: "You can switch from any 1st of the month." Primary button "Continue with <plan>" is disabled until a plan is chosen. No cards.

**DR3-3A Plan on the Billing page (FR-TA-175, DR2-1A; D5).** DR2-1A's order stays. The "This month so far" block starts with one line: "Fixed plan · Change" or "Usage plan · Change"; a scheduled switch replaces it with "Usage plan from 1 Jan · Cancel". "Last month on the other plan: about ₹X" sits under the per-meter detail (Usage) or the allowance detail (Fixed). No plan panel.

**DR3-4A Seat step in the booking flow (FR-BA-013, 1A, 2A; D6).** After the time pick (1A), products with Seat choice Optional or Required open a seat screen: the map fills the screen with a sticky bottom bar. Optional: "Continue with any seat", becoming "Continue with A1" after a tap; for N seats "2 of 3 picked" until all are picked. Required: the bar reads "Pick 1 seat" and stays disabled until picked. The 2A summary shows "Seat A1 · Change" (or "Any available seat · Change"). If a picked seat is taken during OTP, the customer returns to this screen with OTP still verified, no hold started, the lost seat highlighted and the message "A1 was just booked · pick another seat".

Pass 1 (Information architecture): 3/10 → 8/10 after DR3-1A to DR3-4A. Remaining: the builder's screen layout, decided in Pass 6.

**DR3-5A Publish check sheet (FR-TA-068c, US-4.7, US-4.9; D7).** "Publish map" opens a sheet before anything changes. It lists: bookings that will get a seat and the customers who will be notified ("3 bookings will get seats · 3 customers will be notified"), or "No customers affected"; any blocked seats (future bookings on removed or reassigned seats) and short days ("12 Nov: 14 bookings, 12 seats"). Each blocked item has "Move booking", which opens Change seat (FR-TA-068f) in a side panel without leaving the editor. "Publish" is enabled only when nothing is blocked.

**DR3-6A Builder saving, editors and limits (FR-TA-068, FR-TA-068d; D8).** The draft autosaves after each change (short debounce, versioned saves). The header shows "Saved", "Saving…" or "Not saved · Retry"; leaving the page while a save has failed asks the owner to stay. One editor at a time per draft: a second person sees "<name> is editing this draft · View only". At the limits, the disabled control says why: "Maximum 10 maps per location" or "Maximum 40 rows × 60 columns".

**DR3-7A Plan edge states (FR-TA-182, FR-TA-175, US-9.9; D9).** Trial ended without a plan: "Your trial has ended · Choose a plan to keep your site live"; the site stays on the platform subdomain and bookings keep working, with "Your site pauses on <date>"; after 30 days (day 45) the tenant moves to Suspended until a plan is chosen (amended by the eng re-review, D2: TrialEnded state). Manager view: "Fixed plan · Only the Owner can change the plan" (no Change link). Cancelling a scheduled switch asks "Stay on Usage plan?" (or Fixed) with Stay / Keep switch. Fixed plan over an allowance: "WhatsApp allowance used · extra messages come from your wallet · Top up" (per channel).

Pass 2 (Interaction states): 5/10 → 9/10 after DR3-5A to DR3-7A (CEO review 3 Section 11 already covered loading/empty/error for the picker). Remaining: motion feedback, Pass 7.

**DR3-8A Seat-assigned and seat-moved messages (R3-A1, FR-TA-068f, 29A; D10).** In the tenant's voice, naming the seat and why, with the space's WhatsApp as the next step: "Workloft set up its seating: your seat on 12 Nov is A3 (window). Want a different seat? Message us on WhatsApp" (seat moved: "Workloft moved your seat on 12 Nov to A4 ..."). The desk pass updates. No customer self-service seat change at launch; staff use Change seat when asked.

Journey storyboard (Pass 3):

```
  STEP | USER DOES                                   | USER FEELS                 | PLAN SPECIFIES?
  -----|---------------------------------------------|----------------------------|------------------------------------------
  1    | Owner opens Seat maps for the first time    | "This looks like work"     | template picker first (FR-TA-068); DR3-6A autosave
  2    | Picks "Rows of desks", paints aisles        | progress, control          | paint tools; touch model in Pass 6
  3    | Presses Publish map                         | worry: "will this break?"  | DR3-5A check sheet names who is notified
  4    | Customer picks a time, sees the map         | curiosity, choice          | DR3-4A sticky "Continue with any seat"
  5    | Taps A1 by the window, pays                 | ownership: "my seat"       | 2A + "Seat A1 · Change"; pass shows A1
  6    | Earlier customer gets "your seat is A3"     | mild surprise              | DR3-8A why + WhatsApp next step
  7    | Owner's trial ends, chooses a plan          | caution about money        | DR3-2A both prices at their usage
  8    | Owner opens Billing next month              | "what do I owe?"           | DR2-1A order, DR3-3A one plan line
```

Time horizons: 5 seconds, the seat map reads as their own room; 5 minutes, a first map published with no lost work; 5 years, labels never change under customers (Pass 7).

Pass 3 (Journey): 6/10 → 9/10 after DR3-8A and the storyboard.

**DR3-9A Fixed plan wording (FR-MW-002, DR3-1A, DR3-2A, FR-TA-175; D11).** Everywhere the Fixed plan is described: "₹F/month · unlimited website and booking-app traffic · includes <n> WhatsApp, <n> SMS, <n> emails a month" (numbers from the rate card in force). The word "unlimited" never stands alone.

Pass 4 (AI slop): 4/10 → 8/10. Classifier: pricing page = PERSUADE; plan screen, builder, billing = OPERATE; seat picker = OPERATE inside the tenant's booking shell. Hard rejections resolved: two-card grid (DR3-1A), headline without action (DR3-1A "Start free trial"), plan screen cards (DR3-2A). Litmus after fixes: 4 YES (one job per section), 5 YES (no cards), 2 YES for the seat picker; 1, 3, 6 and 7 depend on DESIGN.md (Sprint 0, 22A) and the motion decision (Pass 7).

**DR3-10A Seat-state tokens (FR-BA-013, NFR-A11Y-10, 16A, 23A; D12).** Seat states are fixed semantic tokens in DESIGN.md, not derived from the tenant palette: free = outlined seat; picked = filled with a check mark; booked = filled neutral grey with a person icon; out of service = hatched. Each passes 3:1 against the seat background in light and dark mode. The same tokens are used in the builder preview and the picker on every tenant site. The tenant's brand colour appears only in the sticky bar's button.

Pass 5 (Design system): 3/10 → 6/10. DESIGN.md does not exist yet (Sprint 0, 22A); new components to add to its vocabulary: SeatGrid (builder and picker modes), ToolPalette, PublishCheckSheet, PlanRow, ComparisonTable (pricing). The remaining gap is DESIGN.md itself.

**DR3-11A Seat map builder on phones (FR-TA-068, US-4.6; exception to 26A; D13).** The builder works fully on 390px phones, tablets and desktop. It opens in View mode. A bottom tool bar holds Desk, Cabin, Room, Aisle, Wall, Entrance, Label and Select, with 44px buttons and the active tool highlighted. One finger uses the active tool (tap paints one cell, drag paints many); two fingers pan and zoom; long-press starts an area select (copy, move, clear). Cells are at least 32px at the default zoom. Undo and redo sit in the header next to the save status (DR3-6A). On desktop the same tools sit in a left rail with keyboard shortcuts. 26A stays in force for every other builder screen.

**DR3-12A Seat picker on phones (FR-BA-013, NFR-PERF-07; D14).** The map opens fitted to the painted area (never the empty grid), centred on the largest group of free seats, with a small overview inset showing the visible part. When seats are drawn smaller than 24px, a tap zooms into that area instead of picking; a seat is picked only at a tappable size (44px target including spacing). Landscape is allowed. The List view (NFR-A11Y-10) stays one tap away in the header.

Pass 6 (Responsive and accessibility): 4/10 → 9/10 after DR3-11A, DR3-12A and DR3-10A (icons with every state); NFR-A11Y-10 already covers keyboard, screen reader and the list view.

**DR3-13A Stable seat labels (FR-TA-068a, US-4.6; D15).** Auto-labelling applies only to seats that have never been published. A published label changes only when the owner renames it by hand, and the publish check (DR3-5A) lists the bookings and customers affected. Row letters follow rows that contain seats, not grid rows, and continue Z → AA → AB. Template sizes: Rows of desks = 4 rows × 6 desks with a centre aisle on a 6 × 8 grid; Pods of 4 = 2 × 2 desk pods with aisles on an 8 × 8 grid; Cabins along a wall = 4 cabin blocks of 2 × 2 along the top of a 6 × 10 grid; Meeting room + desks = one 3 × 4 room block plus 2 rows of 5 desks on a 7 × 10 grid; Blank = size entered by the owner. US-4.6's example is corrected to match.

**DR3-14A Change seat on the phone (FR-TA-068f, 26A; D16).** A bottom sheet in three steps: (1) "Move which seat?" when the booking has more than one seat; (2) the list of seats free for every day and time of the booking, grouped by row with tags; (3) a confirm screen showing the exact WhatsApp/email text (DR3-8A) with "Notify customer" on by default. 44px rows, one-handed on 390px. The move is recorded in the booking timeline.

**DR3-15A Rooms and cabins on the map (FR-BA-013, FR-TA-068b, 1A; D17).** Time stays first (1A). After the time pick, room and cabin blocks show free or booked for that time, labelled with name and capacity ("Boardroom · 10"); tapping a free block picks it. The sticky bar defaults to "Continue with any room", using the same bar and states as the desk picker (DR3-4A). No map-first room flow.

**DR3-16A Seat map motion (FR-BA-013, FR-TA-068, NFR-A11Y-07; D18).** Three moments only: a picked seat fills with a 120ms ease-out; a seat lost during OTP pulses its outline twice and scrolls into view; a successful publish shows "Map is live" in the save-status area. Nothing else animates. With prefers-reduced-motion, every change is instant and the lost seat gets a static outline.

Pass 7 (Unresolved decisions): 4 resolved (DR3-13A to DR3-16A), 0 deferred.

- Today on the map and "Seat us together": deferred by CEO review 3 (R3-C4), unchanged.
